- **toc-automata-regex-and-parsing**: Use when reasoning about what a language or matcher can express: automata and the Chomsky hierarchy as a practical design tool, DFAs, NFAs and state machines, what regular expressions actually are and what they cannot do, ReDoS and catastrophic backtracking, and parsing and grammar classes (LL, LR, PEG, ambiguity) with the practical points. - **toc-bbit-expert**: Deep Theory of Constraints + Black Belt in Thinking (BBiT) coach: UDEs → Current Reality Tree → Evaporation Cloud → injections → Future Reality, Prerequisite and Transition Trees, with Excalidraw diagrams; also Done Statements, NCNs, ABC/PMCB behaviour change and CLR logic checks. Use on "think like a BBiT expert", "build a current reality tree", "evaporation cloud", "necessary condition network", "write a done statement". - **toc-beyond-np-space-and-distributed-limits**: Use when the hardness question goes past NP or into distributed systems: the polynomial hierarchy, PSPACE and counting classes, fine-grained complexity and conditional lower bounds (SETH, 3SUM, APSP), space complexity, streaming and sublinear algorithms, and the distributed computing impossibility results — FLP, CAP and consensus — with their honest practical readings. - **toc-one-shot**: TOC 限制理論的一條龍降維版本——把五棵樹、七條文法、三問框架壓縮成六個動詞，從診斷到落地一次走完。輸入一個卡住的系統，先判斷它卡在「流量」還是「邏輯」，然後依序動六個動作：找瓶頸讓全局配合、追因果到一個根、戳破撐住兩難的假設、沙盤推演方案的全部後果、把障礙排成有順序的中期目標、為每個目標設計明天就能做的具體動作。全程繁體中文、直接在對話中輸出、不寫任何檔案。務必在以下情境觸發此 skill：當使用者說「TOC one-shot」應觸發。不要在使用者只想單獨畫一張因果圖時觸發——那用 toc-causal-mesh。不要在使用者只想解一個兩難衝突、找蒸發雲突破點時觸發——那用 toc-evaporating-cloud。不要在使用者想要單一觀點往下鑽時觸發——那用 deep-dive。不要在使用者想要橫向找生成器、降維時觸發——那用 reduce-rank。不要在使用者想要養形尋機立勢的商戰戰略時觸發——那用 sunzi-planning。 ([Joseph-dth/fanbook_article_gen_readme](https://github.com/Joseph-dth/fanbook_article_gen_readme/tree/HEAD/editer_skill_set/toc-one-shot))
